     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * Operation-scoped mutation lock (plan §Concurrency, Lock model).
     8	 * Acquire = atomic non-recursive mkdir of .handoff/lock/; exclusivity comes from
     9	 * EEXIST. Ownership facts live in owner.json; logical session ownership lives in
    10	 * bundle.json, never here. Takeover only when the owner is provably dead on this
    11	 * host — every other contended state refuses, and `force` never changes that.
    12	 */
    13	
    14	export class LockHeldError extends Error {
    15	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    16	    super(message);
    17	    this.name = 'LockHeldError';
    18	  }
    19	}
    20	
    21	export class FencingError extends Error {
    22	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    23	    super(message);
    24	    this.name = 'FencingError';
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	/** @param {string} root */
    29	function lockPaths(root) {
    30	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    31	  return {
    32	    dir,
    33	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
    34	    owner: `${dir}/lock/owner.json`,
    35	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
    36	  };
    37	}
    38	
    39	const ESCAPE_HATCH =
    40	  'Verify manually that no baton process still holds it, then delete .handoff/lock to clear.';
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Inspect the current lock state.
    44	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p
    45	 * @returns {{state: 'free'} | {state: 'torn', cause: string} | {state: 'live'|'dead'|'cross-host', owner: any}}
    46	 */
    47	function inspect(io, p) {
    48	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.lockDir)) return { state: 'free' };
    49	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.owner)) {
    50	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is missing (torn lock metadata)' };
    51	  }
    52	  let owner;
    53	  try {
    54	    owner = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
    55	  } catch {
    56	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is unparseable (torn lock metadata)' };
    57	  }
    58	  if (owner.host !== io.host) return { state: 'cross-host', owner };
    59	  if (io.processAlive(owner.pid, owner.startTime)) return { state: 'live', owner };
    60	  return { state: 'dead', owner };
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} text */
    64	function journalNote(io, p, text) {
    65	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
    66	  // Allocate seq + dedupeKey like every other journal entry (gate-2 fix,
    67	  // reviewer-b finding 2): a keyless, seq-less note poisons journalSeq to NaN
    68	  // on replay and dedupes every later keyless event. We are already inside the
    69	  // lock here, so reading the tail for allocation is race-free.
    70	  let tailSeq = 0;
    71	  try {
    72	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${p.dir}/bundle.json`, 'utf8'));
    73	    if (typeof snap?.journalSeq === 'number') tailSeq = snap.journalSeq;
    74	  } catch {
    75	    // no snapshot yet — journal tail below still counts
    76	  }
    77	  try {
    78	    for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
    79	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
    80	    }
    81	  } catch {
    82	    // no journal yet
    83	  }
    84	  const ts = io.now();
    85	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, {
    86	    seq: tailSeq + 1,
    87	    ts,
    88	    type: 'note',
    89	    dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts, type: 'note', source: 'lock', text }),
    90	    writerId: ['lock', io.host, io.pid].join('-'),
    91	    source: 'lock',
    92	    payload: { text },
    93	  });
    94	}
    95	
    96	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
    97	function publishOwner(io, p) {
    98	  const token = io.newFencingToken();
    99	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.owner, {
   100	    host: io.host,
   101	    pid: io.pid,
   102	    startTime: io.startTime,
   103	    fencingToken: token,
   104	    acquiredAt: io.now(),
   105	    heartbeatAt: io.now(),
   106	  });
   107	  return token;
   108	}
   109	
   110	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
   111	function acquire(io, p) {
   112	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   113	  try {
   114	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   115	    return publishOwner(io, p);
   116	  } catch (err) {
   117	    if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code !== 'EEXIST') throw err;
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   121	  switch (s.state) {
   122	    case 'free':
   123	      // Raced a release between mkdir failure and inspection; retry once. A
   124	      // competitor can win THIS mkdir too (gate-2 fix 12: observed under real
   125	      // barrier-released contention) — that is contention, not corruption.
   126	      try {
   127	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   128	      } catch (err) {
   129	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   130	          throw new LockHeldError('lock was re-acquired by a competitor mid-inspection — transient contention');
   131	        }
   132	        throw err;
   133	      }
   134	      return publishOwner(io, p);
   135	    case 'live':
   136	      throw new LockHeldError(
   137	        `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   138	      );
   139	    case 'cross-host':
   140	      throw new Error(
   141	        `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   142	      );
   143	    case 'torn':
   144	      // During ACQUIRE, torn metadata is usually a competitor caught between
   145	      // its mkdir and owner.json publication — transient by construction, so
   146	      // LockHeldError lets withLock wait it out (gate-2 fix 12). A genuinely
   147	      // crashed publisher exhausts the retry budget and surfaces this same
   148	      // message; recovery semantics (recoverLock) are unchanged: never steal.
   149	      throw new LockHeldError(`${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`);
   150	    case 'dead': {
   151	      // Atomic re-claim (gate-2 iter-2 B1): remove the stale lock dir and WIN
   152	      // a fresh mkdir before publishing. Two contenders racing the same dead
   153	      // owner arbitrate on that mkdir — the loser gets EEXIST and retries —
   154	      // instead of both republishing owner.json into one shared dir. The
   155	      // fencing token remains the write-time backstop.
   156	      io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   157	      try {
   158	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   159	      } catch (err) {
   160	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   161	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor re-claimed the dead lock first — transient contention');
   162	        }
   163	        throw err;
   164	      }
   165	      const token = publishOwner(io, p);
   166	      journalNote(io, p, `lock takeover: took over stale lock from dead pid ${s.owner.pid} (start time ${s.owner.startTime})`);
   167	      return token;
   168	    }
   169	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
   170	    default:
   171	      throw new Error('unreachable lock state');
   172	  }
   173	}
   174	
   175	/**
   176	 * Fence-guarded release: the lock dir is removed only while owner.json still
   177	 * carries OUR token. A holder that paused past a takeover must not delete the
   178	 * competitor's lock on its way out — release is a write like any other.
   179	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} token
   180	 */
   181	function release(io, p, token) {
   182	  try {
   183	    const current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   184	    if (current.fencingToken !== token) return; // a competitor took over — their lock, not ours
   185	  } catch {
   186	    return; // owner metadata gone or torn mid-hold — never guess; leave it for recovery
   187	  }
   188	  io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   189	}
   190	
   191	// A live same-host holder is transient by design — operation-scoped locks are
   192	// held for milliseconds — so contention waits instead of dropping the
   193	// operation (gate-2 fix 12: simultaneous checkpoints must all land). The budget
   194	// is generous (≈15 s): under heavy load a writer may be starved of scheduling
   195	// for seconds, and waiting is strictly safer than silently dropping its work.
   196	// Every other contended state (torn, cross-host) stays fail-fast: waiting
   197	// cannot make those provably safe.
   198	const DEFAULT_RETRY = { attempts: 600, delayMs: 25 };
   199	
   200	/** Synchronous sleep without burning a core: Atomics.wait on a throwaway buffer. */
   201	const sleepMs = (/** @type {number} */ ms) => {
   202	  Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);
   203	};
   204	
   205	/**
   206	 * Acquire → run fn(token) → release (also on throw). Synchronous by design:
   207	 * everything the lock guards is sync fs work. Live-held locks are retried on
   208	 * a bounded budget (override via io.lockRetry = {attempts, delayMs}).
   209	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {(token: string) => any} fn
   210	 */
   211	export function withLock(root, io, fn) {
   212	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   213	  // Jail BEFORE any lock mutation (gate-2 iter-2 B2): a symlinked .handoff or
   214	  // .handoff/lock must never be created into or removed through — that would
   215	  // reach outside the repository. Checked once here, ahead of acquire.
   216	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   217	  if (!safe.ok) throw new Error(`${safe.problem} — refusing to acquire the lock`);
   218	  const retry = io.lockRetry ?? DEFAULT_RETRY;
   219	  /** @type {string} */
   220	  let token;
   221	  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
   222	    try {
   223	      token = acquire(io, p);
   224	      break;
   225	    } catch (err) {
   226	      if (err instanceof LockHeldError && attempt < retry.attempts) {
   227	        sleepMs(retry.delayMs);
   228	        continue;
   229	      }
   230	      throw err;
   231	    }
   232	  }
   233	  try {
   234	    return fn(token);
   235	  } finally {
   236	    release(io, p, token);
   237	  }
   238	}
   239	
   240	/**
   241	 * Fast-abort layer: re-read the published token immediately before a write; a
   242	 * holder that paused past its last check aborts instead of clobbering.
   243	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} token @param {() => any} fn
   244	 */
   245	export function guardedWrite(root, io, token, fn) {
   246	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   247	  let current = null;
   248	  try {
   249	    current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   250	  } catch {
   251	    throw new FencingError('lock owner metadata is gone — fencing check failed; aborting write');
   252	  }
   253	  if (current.fencingToken !== token) {
   254	    throw new FencingError(
   255	      `fencing token changed (held '${token}', current '${current.fencingToken}') — another process took over; aborting write`,
   256	    );
   257	  }
   258	  return fn();
   259	}
   260

+    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync('/repo/.handoff/purge.marker.json'), false);
+    assert.doesNotMatch(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json'], /SECRET-tail/);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('rotation-marker reconciliation respects the repo lock', () => {
+  it('a held lock defers reconciliation with a warning instead of writing un-locked', () => {
+    const marker = JSON.stringify({ kind: 'finalize', startedAt: T0, seq: 0 });
+    const io = makeIo({
+      files: {
+        '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(validBundle()),
+        '/repo/.handoff/rotation.marker.json': marker,
+        '/repo/.handoff/lock/owner.json': JSON.stringify({ host: 'host-A', pid: 777, startTime: 1, fencingToken: 'x' }),
+      },
+      now: T0,
+      processAlive: () => true, // the lock holder is alive
+    });
+    const { warnings } = loadBundle('/repo', io);
+    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync('/repo/.handoff/rotation.marker.json'), true, 'the marker is untouched while the lock is held');
+    assert.ok(warnings.some((w) => /defer/i.test(w)), `deferral is surfaced: ${JSON.stringify(warnings)}`);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('status runs through the recovery ladder', () => {
+  it('a corrupt snapshot with a good .bak still reports the bundle', async () => {
+    const good = validBundle();
+    const io = makeIo({
+      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': 'corrupt{{{', '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json.bak': snapText(good) },
+      now: T0,
+    });
+    assert.equal(await run(['status', '--json'], io), 0, 'status recovers instead of failing on a corrupt snapshot');
+    const env = JSON.parse(io.stdoutText());
+    assert.equal(env.ok, true);
+    assert.equal(env.data.bundleId, good.bundleId);
+  });
+
+  it('no bundle stays exit 1 with the no-bundle envelope', async () => {
+    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/x.txt': 'x' } });
+    assert.equal(await run(['status', '--json'], io), 1);
+    assert.equal(JSON.parse(io.stdoutText()).error.code, 'no-bundle');
+  });
+});
+
+describe('hook errors land in .handoff/log with bounded, metadata-only entries', () => {
+  const PLUGIN_ROOT = '/repo';
+  const hookIo = (files = {}) => {
+    const io = makeIo({ env: { CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT: PLUGIN_ROOT }, stdin: JSON.stringify({ hook_event_name: 'Stop', session_id: 's', secret_payload: 'sk-ant-SUPERSECRET' }), files, now: T0 });
+    io.execFile = () => Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn node ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
+    return io;
+  };
+
+  it('a failing core invocation logs {ts, event, code} — never the payload', async () => {
+    const io = hookIo();
+    assert.equal(await runHook(['Stop'], io), 0, 'still fail-open');
+    const log = io.files()['/repo/.handoff/log/hook-errors.jsonl'];
+    assert.ok(log, 'the error log exists');
+    const entry = JSON.parse(log.trim().split('\n')[0]);
+    assert.equal(entry.event, 'Stop');
+    assert.ok(entry.ts);
+    assert.doesNotMatch(log, /SUPERSECRET/, 'metadata only — raw hook payloads never enter the log');
+  });
+
+  it('retention: at most 50 entries survive a write', async () => {
+    const old = Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, i) => JSON.stringify({ ts: T0, event: `old-${i}`, code: null })).join('\n') + '\n';
+    const io = hookIo({ '/repo/.handoff/log/hook-errors.jsonl': old });
+    await runHook(['Stop'], io);
+    const lines = io.files()['/repo/.handoff/log/hook-errors.jsonl'].trim().split('\n');
+    assert.ok(lines.length <= 50, `retention holds: ${lines.length} entries`);
+    assert.match(lines[lines.length - 1], /"Stop"/, 'the newest entry is present');
+  });
+});
+
+describe('history freeze copies are atomic', () => {
+  it('the frozen snapshot first appears via rename, never as a partial write', () => {
+    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(validBundle()), '/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson': '' }, now: T0 });
+    const stem = rotateJournal('/repo', 'finalize', io);
+    const freezePath = `/repo/.handoff/history/${stem}.json`;
+    assert.ok(io.fs.existsSync(freezePath));
+    const firstAppearance = io.fs.__history.find((/** @type {any} */ h) => freezePath in h.files);
+    assert.ok(firstAppearance, 'the freeze landed');
+    assert.equal(firstAppearance.op, 'renameSync', `the freeze must land atomically (tmp+rename); first seen via ${firstAppearance.op}`);
+  });
+});

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba core/src/util/jail.mjs | sed -n '1,220p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Threat-model guards (plan §Threat model): bundle-borne paths and the managed
     2	// .handoff tree are untrusted input. jailRelPath is the single pure gate every
     3	// repository path passes through before storage or render; checkHandoffTree
     4	// refuses to operate on a managed tree that contains symlinks or resolves
     5	// outside the repository root.
     6	
     7	const NUL = String.fromCharCode(0);
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Normalize an untrusted repository-relative path, or return null when it is
    11	 * unsafe: non-string, empty, NUL bytes, absolute (posix, Windows drive, UNC),
    12	 * or escaping the root via `..`. Backslashes are treated as separators so a
    13	 * Windows spelling cannot smuggle a traversal past a posix check.
    14	 * @param {any} p
    15	 * @returns {string | null}
    16	 */
    17	export function jailRelPath(p) {
    18	  if (typeof p !== 'string' || p.length === 0 || p.includes(NUL)) return null;
    19	  const unified = p.replace(/\\/g, '/');
    20	  if (unified.startsWith('/')) return null;
    21	  if (/^[A-Za-z]:/.test(unified)) return null;
    22	  /** @type {string[]} */
    23	  const out = [];
    24	  for (const seg of unified.split('/')) {
    25	    if (seg === '' || seg === '.') continue;
    26	    if (seg === '..') {
    27	      if (out.length === 0) return null;
    28	      out.pop();
    29	      continue;
    30	    }
    31	    out.push(seg);
    32	  }
    33	  if (out.length === 0) return null;
    34	  return out.join('/');
    35	}
    36	
    37	/** Thrown by store mutations when the managed tree fails the jail check. */
    38	export class UnsafeTreeError extends Error {}
    39	
    40	/**
    41	 * Verify the managed .handoff tree is safe to operate on: the directory itself
    42	 * is not a symlink, its realpath stays under the repository root, and no entry
    43	 * anywhere in the tree is a symlink (lstat walk — never follows links). An
    44	 * absent tree is safe; an unverifiable tree is refused, never trusted.
    45	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    46	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, problem: string}}
    47	 */
    48	export function checkHandoffTree(root, io) {
    49	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    50	  try {
    51	    if (!io.fs.existsSync(dir)) return { ok: true };
    52	    if (io.fs.lstatSync(dir).isSymbolicLink()) {
    53	      return { ok: false, problem: `.handoff is a symlink — refusing to operate through a linked managed tree` };
    54	    }
    55	    const rootReal = io.fs.realpathSync(root);
    56	    const dirReal = io.fs.realpathSync(dir);
    57	    if (dirReal !== `${rootReal}/.handoff`) {
    58	      return { ok: false, problem: `.handoff resolves outside the repository root (${dirReal}) — refusing` };
    59	    }
    60	    // Walk is race-tolerant (gate-2 iter-2): under concurrent atomic writes
    61	    // the tree churns — tmp files, the lock dir, and rotated journals appear
    62	    // and vanish between readdir and lstat. An entry that disappears mid-walk
    63	    // (ENOENT) is normal concurrency, NOT a symlink attack, so it is skipped;
    64	    // only a POSITIVELY observed symlink refuses. The stable top-level checks
    65	    // above (.handoff itself a symlink / realpath escape) stay strict.
    66	    /** @type {string[]} */
    67	    const stack = [dir];
    68	    while (stack.length > 0) {
    69	      const d = /** @type {string} */ (stack.pop());
    70	      /** @type {string[]} */
    71	      let names;
    72	      try {
    73	        names = io.fs.readdirSync(d);
    74	      } catch (e) {
    75	        if (/** @type {any} */ (e)?.code === 'ENOENT') continue; // subdir removed mid-walk
    76	        throw e;
    77	      }
    78	      for (const name of names) {
    79	        const p = `${d}/${name}`;
    80	        let st;
    81	        try {
    82	          st = io.fs.lstatSync(p);
    83	        } catch (e) {
    84	          if (/** @type {any} */ (e)?.code === 'ENOENT') continue; // entry vanished mid-walk
    85	          throw e;
    86	        }
    87	        if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return { ok: false, problem: `${p} is a symlink — refusing (managed-tree jail)` };
    88	        if (st.isDirectory()) stack.push(p);
    89	      }
    90	    }
    91	    return { ok: true };
    92	  } catch (err) {
    93	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    94	    return { ok: false, problem: `managed tree could not be verified (${msg}) — refusing` };
    95	  }
    96	}
    97	
    98	/**
    99	 * Assert form of checkHandoffTree for mutation paths: throws UnsafeTreeError
   100	 * so commands surface the refusal through their existing soft-failure handling.
   101	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   102	 */
   103	export function assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io) {
   104	  const r = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   105	  if (!r.ok) throw new UnsafeTreeError(r.problem);
   106	}
